## Law in force (citations)
- Passes in order, each ending in a fresh-critic `VERDICT: YES` with no BLOCKING fault — RULES §1, §3.
- Frame 0 = an expressive face; never an empty scene, never a back — RULES §4; Build exit (head ≥300 px on 9:16).
- Pure in t (seeded, no Math.random/Date) — RULES §4. Never edit the engine copy — RULES §4/§9.
- PROP GUARD: a prop that jumps on screen fails the film — RULES §4.
- Set bar: shot ≥22% detail / ≤80% flat; film median ≥28% / ≤78% flat; ≤40% of shots under — RULES §5.
- Voice is law — RULES §7. **Conflict named:** repo AGENTS.md (owner's standing word there) says the
  narration file is used *exactly as-is — no trimming, no cleaning; the film is exactly as long as the
  file*. That word governs over §7's pause-tightening step for this run: `voice_owner.wav` is a
  byte-identical copy of the owner's WAV (verified with `cmp`), film end = 68.08 s = file length.
- Format: cartoon-shorts, fps 30, size [1080, 1920] — RULES §10. (60 fps cancelled by the owner.)
- Critic: NEW agent each round, blind-mixed with bar frames, fixed answer form, fix only the
  BIGGEST GAP at its cause — RULES §3 / skill references/critic.md.
- Routes (owner steering 21:59 EET): **storyboard stills on the GitHub fleet; final render on
  Colab (L4)** — after board approval only. API doors all in use with cross-model verification
  at every gate; Gemini API is free-tier Flash-only, 250 req/day — metered, no retry-burning
  (Seat 7 keeps the counter; a dead key is reported immediately — desk key-watch).

## Defect register (from the prior run — must be impossible this time)

  box entry's `fetch: true` fix (prior annotation fix-1795371591203).

## Phase 30 — SH17 render-layer DIAGNOSIS (Layla's option B): the engine does not hold boat_b where the audit holds it — a rot-only key resets its position to the world origin (2026-11-23 ~14:4x EET → complete)

- **Standing order:** diagnosis only — no fix, no camera change, no application. Layla's ruling: option B from phase 29. Method: one engine-side fleet `track` probe on the BASELINE tree (HEAD `bf8fef5`) + code reading of the engine's placement path and the audit's prop-state path + re-verification of every SH17 staging fact against the film's own data. **NO LOCAL RENDERS; Colab LOCKED; nothing applied; the tree is untouched by this phase except this record and the track evidence file.**
- **Fleet track probe (run 45177077929, SUCCESS; bootError null, suiteErrors [], 44/44 frames tracked, spec `track:1178:1222` = t 39.27–40.70, the whole SH17 span plus margins).** Evidence kept at `probes/chunk3/proptrack_sh17_p30.json` (per-frame: the engine's own projection×view matrix, camera position/forward, and every prop's world position as the engine placed it). Analysis by a transient script (created/run/deleted in-phase, /tmp; phase-23/26 pattern).
- **FINDING 1 — where the engine actually puts boat_b (MEASURED, from the track):** boat_b sits at its briefed sea position and settles in place — [2.5, .15, −37] at F1178–F1188, y easing .15→.02 across F1189–F1194 (the ±0.1 s key blend around its 39.70 settle key) — then, across F1196–F1201 (39.87–40.03), it SLIDES shoreward and up-frame through [2.45, .02, −36.27], [2.11, .02, −31.22] (F1197), [1.56, .01, −23.08], [0.94, .01, −13.92], [0.39, 0, −5.78], [0.05, 0, −0.73], and from **F1202 (t = 40.07) to the end of the shot it sits at exactly [0, 0, 0] — the world origin** — including at F1215 (t = 40.50). The slide window is exactly the ±0.1 s blend around boat_b's rot-only key at t = 39.95.
- **FINDING 2 — boat_a has the same fault, one key later (MEASURED):** boat_a settles at [−3.5, .08, −40] at F1197 (blend midpoint of its 39.90 settle key — this is why the F1197 invariant passes in the render), reaches [−3.5, .02, −40] at F1200, then slides across F1201–F1205 and sits at **[0, 0, 0] from F1206 (t = 40.20)** — the blend around its rot-only key at t = 40.10. At F1215 BOTH boats are stacked at the world origin. In the final film both boats therefore vanish from the shore for the last ~0.5 s of SH17 and sit at the village origin for every later frame (placement is pure in t; no later key moves them) — INFERRED from the measured positions + the placement code below; no later shot was probed.
- **FINDING 3 — the projection at F1215 (MEASURED through the engine's own pv matrix, baseline camera):** the engine's camera at F1215 is pos [1.67, 12.54, −29.96] — **identical to the audit's sampled baseline camera** (the brief's figure): the audit's camera model is NOT the divergence. Projecting through that pv: boat_b's ENGINE position [0, 0, 0] has clip w = **−18.48 (behind the camera)**; its mast-top is likewise behind (w = −20.22). Zero pixels is therefore necessary, under any sea camera. Under the phase-29 candidate camera (audit-model arithmetic on the phase-29 line, both eases): the origin sits **12.54 units behind** the camera plane (ahead-dot −12.54, empirical and outCubic alike) — so the candidate render's zero hull pixels are fully explained by placement alone. The phase-29 eye note is consistent: at F1197 under the candidate, boat_b was mid-slide at [2.11, .02, −31.22], ahead of that camera — the tan sliver at the still's bottom-right.
- **FINDING 4 — the mechanism (MEASURED, code):** the engine's placement (`lib/stage.js` prop loop + `propPlace`, `lib/film/props.js` `placeProp`) resolves a prop from its GOVERNING KEY WHOLESALE: placement keys are the keys carrying any of `in/owner/at/rot/drop`; the governing key at t is the last such key ≤ t, with position blended between the previous and governing placements across ±pass (0.1 s) around each key; when a key governs, ONLY that key's fields are passed (base = {}), and the world branch reads `at = P.at || [0, 0, 0]`. **A key that carries `rot` but no `at` therefore resets the prop's position to [0, 0, 0].** boat_b's `{t: 39.95, rot: [0, 0, −11]}` and boat_a's `{t: 40.10, rot: [0, 0, 14]}` are exactly such keys. The AUDIT's `propState` (`tools/audit_staging.mjs`) instead resolves each field INDEPENDENTLY (lastField('at'), lastField('rot')) — it merges the settle key's `at` with the rot key's `rot`, a state the engine never holds. Hence the audit credits boat_b at [2.5, .02, −37] (449 px under the candidate) while the engine holds it at the origin. The audit's projection arithmetic itself is sound (its boat_a readings reproduced in renders at 509/381 px); the wrong input is the prop POSITION for split-field keys. This is the phase-8 audit-model class with the exact seam now named: **field-wise key merge (audit) vs governing-key wholesale with a [0, 0, 0] default (engine)**. The audit also steps `at` without the ±0.1 s blend — a ≤0.06-unit difference at F1197 for boat_a (engine [−3.5, .08, −40] vs audit [−3.5, .02, −40]; projections 6 px apart) — immaterial everywhere except inside a blend whose far endpoint is the origin placement, where it is the whole fault.
- **FINDING 5 — occlusion hypothesis: TESTED AND EXCLUDED (MEASURED arithmetic):** the sea_sheet's rendered surface is a horizontal plane at world y ≈ 0.04 spanning x [−60, 60], z [−112, −32] (see fact 5 below). From the engine's F1215 baseline camera [1.67, 12.54, −29.96], the sightlines to boat_b's audit-position base / geometry-mid / mast-top reach that plane only at segment parameters u = 0.998 / 1.151 / 1.356 — i.e. at or beyond the boat itself; the sheet lies UNDER the boat and cannot cover its body from any camera above it. The only other sheet part, the foam strip (0.035 tall at world y ≈ .06–.095, z = −32 − pull = −68 at pull 36), never intersects the F1215 sightline segment (z spans only −29.96 → −37). Occlusion contributes nothing; the prop is not culled or hidden either — propTrack returns a live position for boat_b at every frame (visible, not farOff): **the engine renders boat_b faithfully — at the origin, behind the sea cameras.**
- **SH17 STAGING FACTS RE-VERIFIED against the film's own data (film.js lines 70–72 + SH17 line 93; audio/clean.json line 15 = 39.28–40.67; words والبحر 39.28 / اتسحب 39.84 / لورا 40.30) — the complete list a corrective re-brief must carry:**
  1. boat_a `at` = SEA+[−3.5, .15, 4] = world **[−3.5, 0.15, −40]**. The brief's stated −37 is WRONG (found phase 29, re-verified: −44 + 4 = −40).
  2. boat_a settle key {39.90, at SEA+[−3.5, .02, 4]} → world [−3.5, 0.02, −40]. The brief's "lowers it to [−3.5, 0.02, −37]" is WRONG by the same slip.
  3. boat_a rot key {40.10, rot [0, 0, 14]} — values as briefed, BUT it is a separate rot-only key: in the engine it moves boat_a to [0, 0, 0] from t = 40.20 (Finding 2). Unbriefed until now.
  4. boat_b `at` = [2.5, 0.15, −37] ✓; settle key {39.70 → [2.5, 0.02, −37]} ✓; rot key {39.95, rot [0, 0, −11]} ✓ as values — with the same unbriefed consequence: boat_b is at [0, 0, 0] from t = 40.07 (Finding 1).
  5. sea_sheet `at` = [0, 0.02, −44] ✓ as a GROUP origin — but the rendered water surface (scenes/props/sea_sheet.js) is a 120×80 plane offset local z −28: world surface centre **[0, ~0.04, −72], spanning z [−112, −32]**, x [−60, 60]. Any brief picturing the sheet centred at −44 (spanning −84..−4) is WRONG; its shoreward edge is z = −32 and the boats float 5–8 units inside that edge.
  6. sea_sheet pull keys ✓ verbatim (0 @ line:15.start = 39.28; 4 @ line:15.word:2 = **39.84** measured; 14 @ 39.90; 36 @ 40.50; 36 @ line:15.end = 40.67). Pull's ONLY rendered effect is the foam strip's z = 12 − pull (local) = world −32 − pull (0 → −32, 14 → −46, 36 → −68). **The water plane does not move, shrink or drain with pull** — the build reads `pull` for the foam only. The recession reads on screen from the foam line and the boats settling, nothing else.
  7. SH17 span 39.28–40.67 ✓; baseline camera line as briefed ✓ (film.js line 93).
  8. Class scan (MEASURED, grep over scenes/film.js, 40 keyed props): `rot` appears in prop keys ONLY on the two boat entries — the split-field trap is exactly boat_a + boat_b in the whole film; no other prop carries a rot key.
- **What this means for cause 8 (facts for the dispatcher; no fix applied or authored here):** the remaining fault is in the FILM DATA, not the camera. No SEA camera can show boat_b at F1215 while the engine holds it at the origin. A corrective round must therefore be a DATA fix first — authored by a seat (the rot must ride on a key that also carries the settled `at`, for BOTH boats) — judged by a fleet track + fleet still, not by the staging audit, which cannot see this class (its field-wise propState merges what the engine keeps separate; whether to amend the audit is the dispatcher's call — recorded here as a known blindness, not amended by this driver). Only after the data holds boat_b at [2.5, 0.02, −37] at F1215 does a camera question remain: under the baseline camera its geometry-mid projects below the frame (engine pv: NDC y −1.17), so a camera component likely remains, and the phase-29 candidate — which centred the audit position at 449 px — becomes live evidence again, to be re-judged on the fixed data.
- **VERDICT — PHASE 30 DIAGNOSIS COMPLETE: mechanism ESTABLISHED with measured evidence.** The audit credits boat_b in frustum at F1215 because it merges key fields the engine resolves wholesale; the engine's governing-key rule sends boat_b (and boat_a) to the world origin at their rot keys, behind every sea camera.
